toṭakkattil milvaṭṭattil pōli paṭṭāḷam niṟuttappaṭṭatu; piṉṉē airōppiya irāṇuvam niṟuttap paṭṭatu. pōlisum irāṇuvamum aṇi aṇiyāka niṉṟu kāval purintamiyāl ātitirāviṭar cilarum vēṟucilarum millukkuḷ cellalāyiṉar. avar kāli millukkuḷ pōkum pōtum, māli tirumpum pōtum pōlisum irāṇuvamum purinta kāval kōlatti vēṭikki pārkkac ceṉṉi vācikaḷ tiraṇṭu varuvārkaḷ.
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa toḻilāḷar millukkuḷ pōkātu virataṅ kāttamiyāl veḷiyūrkaḷiliruntu āṭkaḷ tiraṭṭap paṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaṭkeṉat taṉiyiṭamum mil atikārikaḷāl amikkappaṭṭatu.
nāḷukkunāḷ karuṅkālikaṭkum (Black Legs) vēli niṟuttak kārarkkum palavitac caṇṭikaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. katti veṭṭukkaḷum, veṭi vīccukkaḷum, vīṭukaḷil tī vippum, piṟa mūrkkac ceyal kaḷum aṭikkaṭi naṭipeṟṟaṉa.
kalakamum, kuḻappamum pala iṭaṅkaḷil paraviṉa. toḻilāḷar mītu vaḻakkukaḷum vāraṇṭukaḷum maliyalāyiṉa. caṅka niliyatti irāṇuvam kāval pūṇṭatu; niliyam cōtikkappaṭṭatu. caṅkattilē kāliyilum māliyilum toḻilāḷarkku ahimsā tarma upatēcañ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu.
nāṭu kaṭattal
yāṉ appoḻutu toḻilāḷar tolliyil mūḻkiyirun tālum kāṅkira toṇṭi viṭuttēṉilli. tamiḻnāṭṭil cattiyākirakat toṇṭic ceytē vantēṉ. cila iṭaṅkaḷil eṉṉuṭaṉ vantavarkkumaṭṭum 144 vaḻaṅkappaṭum; eṉakku vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatilli. aḵtu eṉakkum maṟṟavarkkum viyappūṭṭum. uriya kāraṇam palavāṟu collappaṭṭatu, palavita vatantikaḷ eḻuntu eḻuntu maṟintaṉa. toḻilāḷar kuḻappaṅ kāraṇamāka aracāṅkam tiru.vi.ka.vi viḻuṅkappōkiṟatu. avarukkup perun taṇṭaṉi kiṭikkap pōkiṟatu. ataṉāl avar pōkumiṭaṅ kaḷil 144 vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatilli eṉṟa vatanti maṭṭum nilittu ulavalāyiṟṟu. ataṉi valiyuṟutta, lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ nīlakiriyiṉiṉṟum varappōkiṟār; vantatum tiru.vi.ka.viyum avaric cārnta cilariyum nāṭu kaṭattap pōkiṟār eṉṟoru vatanti toṭarntu piṟantatu. atu kāṭṭut tīppōl ceṉṉi muḻuvatum paraviṟṟu.  naṇpar palarum eṉṉik kaṇṭu kaṇṭu vicāritta vaṇṇamiruppar. akkālattil iraṭṭi āṭciyil pataviyil vīṟṟiruntavar jaṭi kaṭciyār.
lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ (4-7-1921) nīlakiriyiṉiṉṟum ceṉṉi cērntu mutalil karuṅkālikaḷik kaṇṭaṉar; piṉṉē pala talivar kaḷi varavaḻittup pēciṉar.
aṭuttanāḷ māli 6 maṇikkuk kavarṉar vīṭṭukku varumāṟu eṉakku aḻippu vantatu. ataṟkiṇaṅki yāṉ kavarṉar māḷikikkuc ceṉṟēṉ. eṉakku muṉṉarē tōḻarkaḷ cakkaric ceṭṭiyār, i.el.aiyar, jalilkāṉ, aptul hakīm ākiya nālvar aṅkē pōntiruntaṉar. 6 maṇikku mantirimār valatu pakkamum nirvāka aṅkattavarkaḷ iṭatu pakkamum iruppa, lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ naṭuvilē amarntār. mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi mariyātikaḷ naṭantaṉa. eṅkaḷ aivariyum lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ pārttu, 

toḻilāḷar vēli niṟuttaṅ kāraṇamākac ceṉṉiyil veṭṭuk kuttukkaḷum veṭikaḷum vīṭukaḷil tīvippum naṭantu varukiṉṟaṉa. avikaṭ kellām mūlakāraṇar nīṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṟēṉ. amiti kākkum poṟuppu ṅkattukkuṇṭu...... eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. eṅkaḷil oruvar nāṅkaḷ kāraṇarallar eṉṟaṉar; maṟṟoruvar nītimaṉṟam irukkiṟatē eṉṟaṉar; iṉṉoruvar vicāraṇi vēṇṭum  eṉṟaṉar; vēṟoruvar toḻilāḷar caṅkattukkum eṉakkuñ campantamē illi. yāṉ tavaṟāka aḻikkappaṭṭēṉ eṉṟaṉar; yāṉ, ellārkkum niyāyat tīrppunāḷ irukkiṟatu eṉṟēṉ. niyāyat tīrppu nāḷ eṉṟatu lārṭ villiṅṭaṉi uṟuttiyatu pōlum. lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ ēṟa iṟaṅkap pārttu, poḻutākiṟatu; yāṉ uṅkaḷi eccarikki ceyyavē aḻittēṉ. iṉit tīvippu mutaliya _rkkacbcayalkaḷeṭakkumāṉālÚṅkaḷeṭufṭattappaṭuvīrkaḷvṉṟumḻuttamākamṟintār.eh§fŸ Éṭipeṟṟuåṭupōycncarntōm.m‹¿uî, ṭākṭar naṭēca mutaliyār eṉṉik kaṇṭu, lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ eṅkaḷi nāṭukaṭatta uṟuti koṇṭē ceṉṉi nōkkiṉareṉṟum, avar sar.pi.tiyākarāja ceṭṭi yāriyum talimi amiccar paṉakal rājāviyum kalantu ālōcitta pōtu, avviruvarum nāṭu kaṭattuñ ceyalāl tamatu kaṭcikkuk keṭṭa peyar uṇṭākumeṉṟum, ottuḻiyāmiyum maliyāḷak kuḻappamum kaṉaṉṟu kotittuvarum vēḷiyil nāṭu kaṭattalum nikaḻumāyiṉ nakarattil kuḻappam perukumeṉṟum, tām (paṉakal) pataviyiṉiṉṟum vilakalum nēralāmeṉṟum nilimiyi viḷakkiya piṉṉar eṅkaḷi eccarikki ceytuviṭak kavarṉar eṇṇalāyiṉareṉṟum urittār. ceṭṭiyārum paṉakalum eṉakku naṉmi ceyyavilli; tīmi ceytaṉar eṉṟu colli ṭākṭari aṉuppiṉēṉ.
vaḻakkukaḷ
toḻilāḷar mītu pōlisārāl toṭarappeṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷi yellām virittuk kūṟutal anāvaciyam. iraṇṭu vaḻakkukkaḷiṉ tīrppukkaḷ (2,5-8-1921) pattirikikaḷil virivāka veḷivantaṉa. attīrppukkaḷ potumakkaḷiṉ anutāpattit toḻilāḷar mītu tiruppiṉa. aniyāyam makkaḷukku naṉku viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
eyiliṅ kūṭṭa vicāraṇi
tī vippu mutaliya mūrkkac ceyalkaḷi vicāraṇi puriya aracāṅkattāl oru kūṭṭam amikkappaṭṭatu. talivar jaṭi eyiliṅ; aṅkattavar iruvar. avar sar vēṅkaṭarattiṉamum karnūl vakkīl naracimmāccāriyārumāvar. vicāraṇi (10-8-1921il) toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. caṅkat talivaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil cāṉṟu kūṟa yāṉ aḻikkappaṭṭēṉ. avvaḻippukku yāṉ iṇaṅka villi. iṇaṅkal iyalāteṉṟu patil viṭuttēṉ. atil kalaka nikaḻcci potumakkaḷip poṟuttateṉṟum, ataṟkum ceṉṉit toḻilāḷar caṅkattukkum evvitat toṭarpum illi eṉṟum,  piratama pirasiṭeṉsi mājiṭirēṭṭāl aḷikkappeṟṟa iraṇṭu tīrppukkaḷāl kalaka mūlam naṉku viḷaṅkukiṟateṉṟum, iṉṉum cila vaḻakkukaḷ nītimaṉṟattil vicāraṇiyiliruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, eyiliṅ kūṭṭa vicāraṇiyil kalantu koḷḷac caṅkam virumpavilli eṉṟum kuṟikkappeṟṟaṉa. 1eyiliṅ kamiṭṭiyār tamatu aṟikkiyiṉ tōṟṟuvāyil oru cila kuṟippup poṟittu eṉatu kaṭitattiyum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.
tuppākki muḻakkam
ceṉṉi aracāṅkattiṉ ēvutaṟkiṇaṅkap pōlī kamiṣaṉar oru kaṭṭaḷi piṟappittaṉar. atu varumāṟu:-
ji. allatu vēppēri pōlī ṭēṣaṉ, ec. pōlī ṭēṣaṉic cērnta mūlik kottaḷam, viyācarpāṭi allatu vaṇṇārappēṭṭi pōlī ṭēṣaṉ ākiya ivvellikkuḷ aṭaṅkiyirukkum jaṉaṅkaḷukkut terivippatu eṉṉa veṉṟāl:-
camīpattil naṭipeṟṟa tī vipattukkaḷip paṟṟiyum, amitiyiṉmiyip paṟṟiyum aracāṅkattār peritum kavaṉittu varukiṉṟaṉar. aracāṅkattār immātiriyāṉa kuḻappaṅkaḷellām niṉṟu viṭutaṟkuriya kāriyaṅkaḷiyum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. caṭṭattiyum amitiyiyum nili peṟuttutaṟkāka aracāṅkattār avaciyamāyc ceyyat takkaṉavaṟṟic ceyvar.
caṭṭavirōtamāṉa ellāk kūṭṭaṅkaḷum kalikkap paṭum. kalakatti uṇṭākka muyalkiṟavar mītum, āyutaṅkaḷ vittirukkiṟavarmītum, kōpatti yuṇṭākkakkūṭiya ceykikaḷic ceykiṟavar mītum, aracāṅkattār kaṭumiyāṉa muṟikaḷik kiyāḷvar. aracāṅkattār tammāl kūṭiyavari amitiyā yirukkum jaṉaṅkaḷukkup pātukāppu aḷippar. avaciyam ēṟpaṭṭāl pātukāppuk kāka avviṭattil pōlīpaṭi niṟuttappaṭum. ataṉ celavi av viṭattiluḷḷavar koṭuttal vēṇṭum.
ikkaṭṭaḷiyiṉ uḷḷak kiṭakki uḷḷaṅki nellik kaṉiyeṉa viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
uṇaviṉṟip paṭṭiṉiyāl vāṭṭamuṟṟuk kiṭanta toḻi lāḷarkkut tuppākki uṇavu - kuṇṭu uṇavu - aḷikkappaṭṭatu (29-8-1921) ēḻit toḻilāḷar mītu tuppākkip pirayōkañ ceyyappaṭṭatu. eḻuvar iṟantaṉar; nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉavar kāya muṟṟaṉar. iṟantavaruḷ oruvar peṇmaṇi, tuppākki vēṭṭiṉiṭi avar purinta vīrac ceyalukkuriya niṉivukkuṟi eṉṟu ceṉṉi amikkumō aṟikilēṉ. ēḻimakkaḷ tiyākam maṟakkappaṭukiṟatu.  ēḻu piṇaṅkaḷum ceṉṉiyil ūrvalam vanta kāṭciyit tōḻar ciṅkāravēl ceṭṭiyār varuṇittu eḻutiya kaṭṭuri carittira ulakukkuriyatu. mēlum tuppākkip pirayōkaṅ kaḷ akṭōpar 15lum, cepṭampar 19lum nikaḻntaṉa. iruvar māṇṭaṉar; cilar kāya muṟṟaṉar.
pōlisār cārpil uyir tuṟantavar iruvar. oruvar heṭkāṉṭepil; maṟṟoruvar cārjeṇṭ. avviruvarum toḻilāḷar eṟikuṇṭāl kollappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟu collap paṭṭatu. acceyti uṟutippaṭavilli.
kuḻappa nikaḻccikaḷ pala eṉṉuḷḷattiṉiṉṟum mūṇṭeḻuntu varukiṉṟaṉa. avikaṭkeṉa oru taṉinūl eḻututal vēṇṭum. kuḻappattip paṟṟi nakara paripālaṉa capiyilum, caṭṭa capiyilum vivātaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. avikaḷil tiru. o.taṇikācalam ceṭṭiyār pēciya 1pēccukkaḷil pala uṇmikaḷ curuṅkiya muṟiyil viḷaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
muṭivu
vēli niṟuttam eppaṭi muṭintatu? rājā sar. irāmacāmi mutaliyār paṅkaḷā veḷiyil kūṭiya kūṭṭattil sar.pi.tiyākarāja ceṭṭiyār nikaḻttiya irāja tantirac coṟperukkil mūḻkit toḻilāḷar palar vēlikkut tirumpiṉar. sar.pi.ceṭṭiyār, mātaṅkaḷ āṟāyiṉa. iṉip piṭivātam vēṇṭā. uṅkaḷukku irāja tantiram vēṇṭum. milkaḷil putu āṭkaḷ nirappappaṭu kiṟārkaḷ. putu āṭkaḷ toḻilip payiṉṟu viṭuvārkaḷāyiṉ uṅkaḷukkuk kaṣṭam viḷiyum. pōli kāvaliyum, irāṇuvak kāvaliyuṅ kaṭantu avarkaḷit taṭuttal iyalātu. āṉāl avarkaḷi oru vaḻiyil appuṟap paṭuttal kūṭum. nīṅkaḷellārum vēlikkuc cella uṟutik koṇṭāl avarkaḷ vīṭṭukkaṉuppap paṭuvārkaḷ. ātalāl, nīṅkaḷ uḷḷē pōṅkaḷ. naṉmiyē viḷiyum. caṅkam cākātu eṉṟu pēciya pēccu av vēḷiyil toḻilāḷarkku ucitamākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. sar.pi.ceṭṭi yār jōciyam peritum palittatu.
iraṇṭu
toḻilāḷar iyakkatti muḷiyilēyē kiḷḷi eṟiyac ceyyappeṟṟa cūḻccikaḷuḷ ciṟantaṉavāka uḷḷa iraṇṭu iṅkuk kuṟikkappeṟṟaṉa. oṉṟu vaḻakku; maṟṟoṉṟu nāṭu kaṭattum eccarikki. piṉṉiyatiṉ aṭippaṭi ilāpattil mikac ciṟu urimi toḻilāḷarkku vēṇṭumeṉṟu pēcappaṭṭami; muyaṉṟami aṉṟu, veṟum pēccukku ettuṇi ettuṇi etirppu! etirppāl viti paṭṭuppōy viṭṭatō? viti muḷiviṭṭē varukiṟatu. ataṟkuriya oḷiyum kāṟṟum nīrum paṭarntu, vīci, pāyntē varukiṉṟaṉa. avvoḷiyiyum kāṟṟi yum nīriyum vaṟṟac ceyyum āṟṟal etaṟku milli; evarkku milli.
intiyāvil mutal mutal tōṉṟiya ceṉṉit toḻilāḷar caṅka nikaḻccikaḷ iṉṉuñ cila uḷa. avaṟṟi innūṟkaṇ virippatu poruttamākātu. avaṟṟi eṉṉāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa tiru.vi.ka. vāḻkkik kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka. 1947ām āṇṭu nikaḻcci carittirattil patiyattakkatu. ataṟkeṉa oru taṉi nūl eḻutappaṭalām.
toḻiṟ kāṅkira
ciṟu ciṟu muyaṟcikaḷ oṉṟupaṭṭu araciyal viṭutalikkuk kāṅkirasāka mukiḻttami pōlat toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟu ciṟu muyaṟcikaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟi akila intiyat toḻiṟcaṅkak kāṅkirasāka mukiḻttaṉa. mutal kāṅkira (31-10-1920) lālā lajapatirāy talimiyil pampāyil kūṭiyatu.
1929ām āṇṭil toḻiṟ caṅkak kāṅkira paṇṭita javaharlāl nēru talimiyil kūṭiṟṟu. akkūṭṭattil piḷavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. oru kūṭṭam mitavātattil cikkuṇṭatu. maṟṟoṉṟu camatarmattiṉ vayappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu mitavātam kuṟintum kulintum varukiṟatu.
taṟkāla nili
toḻiṟcaṅka iyakkam tōṉṟi ēṟakkuṟiya muppatu āṇṭuka ḷākiṉṟaṉa. ittuṇi nāḷ evvaḷavō kāriyaṅkaḷic cātit tirukkalām. iyakkam toṭakkattil naṉṉiliyiliruntatu. piṉṉē nāḷukku nāḷ iṟukalāyiṟṟu. annāḷil talivarka ḷukkuḷ oṟṟumi nilaviyatu; avarkaḷāl naṭattappeṟṟa caṅkaṅ kaḷukkum oṟṟumi nilaviyatu; piṉṉē talivarkaḷukkuḷ piṇakku eḻuntatu; caṅkaṅkaḷilum piṇakku eḻuntatu.
araciyal amippukkaḷil toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷukkeṉṟum toḻilāḷarkkeṉṟum cila pīṭaṅkaḷ otukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟil talivarkaḷ amartaṟkuc ceyyappaṭum muyaṟcikaḷil avarkaḷuk kuḷ piṇakku nērum. appiṇakkuc caṅkaṅkaḷilum pukuntu avaṟṟi nācappaṭuttum.
patavi māyi caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ oṟṟumiyik kulittatōṭu maṟṟoru māyiyum avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumiyik kulittatu. atu jiṉivā māyi. jiṉivā cellat talivarkaḷukkuḷ pōṭṭi eḻum. appōṭṭiyāl viḷiyum maṉattāṅkalkaḷ caṅkaṅ kaḷit tākkum.
toḻilāḷar caṅkaṅkaḷ peritum patavi māyikkum jiṉivā māyikkum iriyāyiṉa. ammāyikaḷiṉ poruṭṭuc caṅkaṅ kaḷ ōmpappaṭṭaṉa. toḻilāḷar nalaṉukkeṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷ ōmpappaṭuvatu arumiyākiyatu.
talivarkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar kalantu vāḻa virumpukiṉ ṟārilli; taṉittaṉiyē vāḻa virumpukiṉṟaṉar. it talivar kaḷik koṇṭa caṅkaṅkaḷukkuḷ eṅṅaṉam oṟṟumi uṇṭākum? mutalāḷikaḷ koṭumiyālum - aracāṅka aṭakku muṟiyālum - kuliyāta caṅkaṅkaḷum, talivarkaḷ piṇakkāl kuliyuṅ kāṭciyi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṅkira āṭci
kāṅkira āṭci toḻiṟ kāṅkirasukku ākkan tēṭa villi; ati oṭukkavē muyalkiṟatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ippoḻutu akila intiya tēciya kāṅkira eṉṟōr amippu vallapāy paṭṭēl ācipeṟṟut tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. atu paḻiya toḻiṟ kāṅkirasi aḻippataṟkeṉṟu tōṉṟiyateṉpatu paṭēliṉ pēccāl uṇaralākum. paḻiya toḻiṟ kāṅkirasil potu uṭimik kaṭci ākkam peṟṟuḷḷamiyāl, putut toḻiṟ kāṅkira tōṟṟattukku avaciyam nērntateṉṟu paṭēl pēciṉar. putiya amippu, kāṅkira kāriyak kūṭṭattiṉ ātaraviyum peṟṟuviṭṭatu. kāṅkirakārar paḻiya amippi vēṭṭiyāṭat tuvaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. putu amippu, mutalāḷikaḷ cārpil tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭateṉṟu nāṭu muḻuvatum pēcap paṭukiṟatu.
iḷiñar 
innerukkaṭiyāṉa nērattil poruḷātāra nuṭpatti naṉku uṇarnta iḷiñarkaḷ toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷil cērntu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷāl poruḷātāra nuṭpam piracārañ ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. iṉit toḻilāḷarkaḷ nalluṇarvu peṟṟu oṟṟumiyuṭiyavarkaḷākalām.
vivacāyac caṅkam
toḻilāḷar iyakkam iṉṉum nāṭṭil naṉku parava villi. aḵtu ippoḻutu toḻiṟcālikaḷil vēli ceyvōrik koṇṭa caṅka aḷavil niṟkiṟatu. atu mēlum maṟṟa maṟṟat toḻilāḷariṭaṅkaḷilum ūṭuruvip pāytal vēṇṭum; ciṟappāka vivacāyikaḷiṭattil pukuvatu nallatu.
namatu nāṭṭil perumpāṉmiyōr vivacāyikaḷ. avarkaḷē nāṭṭiṉ mutukelumpāyiruppavarkaḷ. aṟarkaḷi mikac cilar oṭukki āṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ viṭutali, nāṭṭiṉ viṭutaliyākum; poruḷātāra viṭutaliyākum, ākavē poruḷātārap piracāram peritum vivacāyikaḷiṭip paravutal vēṇṭum. vivacāyikaḷiṭip poruḷātāra ñāṉam perukum nāḷē nāṭṭukkuriya naṉṉāḷeṉka. vivacāyikaḷukkup poruḷā tāra ñāṉatti yūṭṭātu, avarkaḷiṭi atiyum itiyum pēci, veṟum aṟivaṟṟa āvēcatti eḻuppuvatu avarkaḷi ēmāṟṟuvatākum. 
innāḷil vaṭa intiyāvil uḻavar caṅkaṅkaḷ, ākkam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. teṉṉintiyāvilum accaṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. nāṭu muḻuvatum uḻavar caṅkam perukuvatu ciṟappu. uḻavar iyakkatti oṭukka nāṭṭuk kāṅkira aracu muṉintu niṟkiṟatu. potujaṉap pātukāppuc caṭṭameṉṟu oṉṟu niṟuvappeṟṟuḷḷatu. atu rulaṭ caṭṭattiṉuṅ koṭiyatu. accaṭṭam toḻiliyakkat talivarkaḷi viḻuṅki varukiṟatu. kāṅkira āṭci mutalāḷi āṭciyāki varutal varuntattakkatu.
kāṅkirasil toḻiliyakkam
iṉit toḻiliyakkam caṅka aḷavil niṟṟalākātu. toḻiṟcaṅka iyakkam toḻiliyakkattiṉ oru paṭi, toḻiliyakkam appaṭiyilēyē niṉṟu viṭṭāl ataṉāl oṉṟum ceytal iyalātu. toḻiliyakkam kāṅkira iyakkamātal vēṇṭum; nāṭṭiyakkamātal vēṇṭum; āyiṉ poruḷātāra viṭutali tāṉē uṇṭākum.
nāṭṭiṉ viṭutalikkeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu kāṅkira. kāṅkira viṭutalikkup pāṭupaṭṭatu. nāṭu anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutali aṭintatu. itu muḻu viṭutaliyākumā? anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟu maṭṭum viṭutali aṭintu, oru ciṟu kūṭṭam iṉpuṟa, maṟṟoru peruṅkūṭṭatti vēliyiṉṟit tiṇṭāṭac ceytu, paṭṭiṉiyālum paciyālum atit tuṉpuṟuttuvatu muḻu viṭutaliyākātu. muḻu viṭutaliyāvatu, anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutali aṭivatuṭaṉ vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam eppakutiyilum tōṉṟāmal nāṭṭāraṉivarum ētō oruvitat toḻil ceytu nalvāḻkki naṭātti iṉpuṟat tuṇipurivatu. itaṟkuc celvam oru pakkam peruki maṟṟoru pakkam arukāta vāṟu kākkavalla āṭcimuṟi tēvi. atuvē camatarma āṭci eṉpatu.
muṉṉē kāṅkira āṭci pala mākāṇaṅkaḷil iraṇṭari āṇṭu naṭipeṟṟatu. akkālattil mantirimār camatarma āṭci kālkoḷḷavāvatu muyaṉṟaṉarā? cāmrājya āṭcimuṟiyi ōmpuvatilēyē avartam muyaṟci ceṉṟatu. avartam āṭciyil vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam perukiṟṟā? arukiṟṟā? nāṭu patiliṟuppatāka, mīṇṭum kāṅkira, āṭci ēṟṟatu. eṉṉa ceytatu? potujaṉap pātukāppeṉṟu urimiyik kallavalla oru koṭuñ caṭṭañ ceytatu. ataṉāl camatarmatti niṟuvavalla toḻiliyakkam paṭum pāṭṭi nāṭaṟiyum.
ippoḻutu nāṭu oruvita viṭutaliyaṭintatu. ati muḻu viṭutaliyākkum muyaṟci nāṭṭil eḻutal vēṇṭum. muḻu viṭutalikku nāṭu camatarmamayamātal vēṇṭum. kāṅkirasum toḻiliyakkamum oṉṟiṉālaṉṟi nāṭu camatarma mayamākātu. ākavē, toḻilāḷar iyakkattik kāṅkirasil oṉṟac ceyya nāṭṭavar muyalvārāka.
camatarmam
camatarmam eṉṟatuñ cilar añcukiṉṟaṉar; cilar atip puli karaṭi ciṅkameṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. atu tavaṟu. camatarma tattuvattip pakuttaṟivāl ārāyntu cintittu oru muṭivukku varutalē aṟivuṭimi. camatarma virōtikaḷ - cuyanalakkārarkaḷ - piracāratti maṭṭuṅ kēṭṭu añcuvatu aṟivuṭimiyākātu.
camatarmattip paṟṟi itukāṟum palatiṟak karuttukkaḷ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. akkaruttukkaḷaṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷ peritum mēl nāṭṭavarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭavi. intiya aṟiñar cilarum camatarmattip paṟṟip pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṉṟaṉar. appēccum eḻuttum peritum mēlnāṭṭu nulkaḷiṉ etirolikaḷākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. veṟum nūlulakil maṭṭum ulavi oru nāṭṭit tirutta muyalvatāl nalam viḷiyātu. cila camayam tīmi viḷiyiṉum viḷiyum.
āṉṟōriṭattiruntu kālattukkēṟṟa tattuvam piṟakkum. attattuvattiyoṭṭi avvannāṭṭu iyaṟki araṇ ceyyum muṟiyil tiṭṭaṅ kōlic cīrtiruttañ ceyvatu araciyal ñāṉamākum. oru nāṭṭu muṟi maṟṟoru nāṭṭu iyaṟkikkup poruntiyumirukkum; poruntāmalumirukkum; orupuṭip poruntiyum maṟṟoru puṭip poruntāmalumirukkum. oṉṟi nāṭṭiṭi nuḻippataṟku muṉṉar, atu nāṭṭiṉ iyaṟkikkup porunti varumā vārātā eṉṟu ārāyntu teḷinta piṉṉarē ati nuḻikka muyalvatu aṟivukku aḻaku.
paḻamperuṅ kālattil ulakam iyaṟkiyōṭiyintu vāḻntamiyāl atu camatarma vaḻiyil iyaṅki vantatu. piṉṉē ceyaṟki vāḻkki mutira mutirac camatarmam cāyntu cāmrājyam eḻalāyiṟṟu. ikkāla ulakam cāmrājya āṭciyāl aluttuk kaḷittu nalintu melintu piṇipaṭṭut tuṉpuṟukiṟatu. ataṉāl ulakam paḻiya camatarmatti nāṭā niṟkiṟatu.
paḻaṅkālattil nilam makkaḷukkup potuvākavē payaṉ paṭṭu vantatu. nāḷaṭivil ataṉ potumi māṟi atu taṉi maṉitar uṭimiyāyiṟṟu. perumpāṉmiyōrkku nila urimi aṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. aracarkaḷum, jamīṉtārikaḷum, mirācutārar kaḷum, celvarkaḷum nilamuṭiyavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. maṟṟavar avarkaḷiṭattil vēli ceytu kūlipeṟṟup piḻippavarāyiṉar.
paṇṭi nāḷil makkaḷ kittoḻilkaḷ ceytu vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. annāḷil mutalāḷi toḻilāḷi vēṟṟumi eḻa villi. mutal toḻil aṟṟa camūkamē nilaviyatu. piṉṉē iyantira kālan tōṉṟiṟṟu. iyantirattāl uṟpatti atikam eṉṟu colla vēṇṭuvatilli. mutalāḷikaḷ iyantiraṅkaḷik koṇṭu uṟpattikaḷip perukkat taṅkaṭkut tēviyāṉa āṭkaḷi amarttik kūlikoṭuttu varalāṉārkaḷ. uṟpattic cātaṉaṅka ḷellām mutalāḷi vayappaṭṭuk kiṭattal kaṇkūṭu.
īriṉam
ippoḻutu ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷ iṉatti iru kūṟākap pirikkalām. oṉṟu mutalāḷi iṉam; maṟṟoṉṟu toḻilāḷi iṉam. iraṇṭil mutalāḷi iṉam āḷvatāyum, toḻilāḷi iṉam āḷappaṭuvatāyum irukkiṉṟaṉa.
mutalāḷi āṭcikku muṉṉē pirapukkaḷ āṭci naṭi peṟṟatu. pirapukkaḷ āṭcikku muṉṉē maṉṉarāṭci naṭi peṟṟatu. maṉṉarāṭci eppaṭip piṟantatu?
ovvoru kuṭumpattilum ovvoru talivar iruntaṉar. kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērnta oṉṟu kirāmam. kuṭumpat talivarellāñ cērntu kirāmat talivarit terinteṭuttaṉar. kirāmaṅka ḷālāyavi kōṭṭam mutaliyaṉa. kirāmat talivarkaḷ oṉṟu paṭṭuk kōṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟukkut talivarkaḷi ēṟpaṭutti ṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kūṭit taṅkaḷukku oru talivari niyamittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. avarē maṉṉarāyiṉar.
ittalivar palarum, toṭakkattil kaṭavuḷiṭattu uṟutiyum aṉpum uṭiyavarāy, jīvakāruṇya muṭiyavarāy, tamakkeṉa vāḻātu maṟṟavarkkeṉa vāḻvōrāy, nila pulaṅkaḷit tarmakarttarāka niṉṟu kāppavarāy, viḷivu ellārkkum payaṉpaṭat tuṇi ceyvōrāyt toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉar. nāḷaṭivil talivarkaḷ nilimi māṟiviṭṭatu. avarkaḷ nilapulaṅkaḷi atikārattāl taṅkaḷuṭimikaḷākkik koṇ ṭārkaḷ. nāḷukkunāḷ maṉṉarāṭciyil koṭuṅkōl pukuntatu eṉṟu curuṅkac collalām. avvāṭci cāykkap paṭṭatu. evarāl? pirapukkaḷāl. pirapukkaḷ āṭci, mutalāḷikaḷāl kaviḻkkappaṭṭatu. cila kālamāka mutalāḷi āṭci cāmrājyamāyt talivirittāṭukiṟatu.
cāmrājyatti vīḻttuṅ kiḷarcciyun tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu ēṉ? iyaṟkiyi nōkkik kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. cāmrājyattāl ulakam peṟṟuvarum anupavam eṉṉa? kuḻappam eṉṟu oru  collāl patiliṟukkalām.
iyaṟkik kūṟukaḷellām potumiyil makkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu. potumi, makkaḷukkuḷ kuḻappam nikaḻātavāṟu kāttu varuntaṉmi vāyntatu. ākavē, iyaṟkik kūṟukaḷiṉ nōkkattukkut tuṇi ceyyavalla aṟa āṭci naṭipeṟutal vēṇṭum. illiyēl, maṟakkuḻappam nikaḻntu koṇṭē irukkum. potumi aṟattukku māṟupaṭṭa āṭci yāl viḷintuvarun tīṅkukaḷip palapaṭak kūṟalām. avaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟu ciṟappākak kuṟikkattakkaṉa. oṉṟu vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam; iṉṉoṉṟu pōrāṭṭam; maṟṟoṉṟu oḻukkak kēṭu.
nila pulaṅkaḷum toḻiṟcālikaḷum maṟṟa uṟpatti tāpaṉaṅ kaḷum taṉi maṉitar uṭimiyāṉapaṭiyāl, avar tam toḻiṉ muṟikaṭkut tēviyāṉa āṭkaḷi maṭṭum vittu vēli vāṅkuvōrāyiṉar. maṟṟavarkku vēli kiṭippatilli. vēli yillāt tiṇṭāṭṭattāl paci paṭṭiṉi pañcam akāla maraṇam kuḻappam mutaliyaṉa nikaḻtal iyalpu. vēliyillātār kiḷarcciyi oṭukkutaṟkeṉṟu mutalāṭciyiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷum, pōlisum, irāṇuvamum, pattirikikaḷum, iṉṉa piṟavum kāttuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
nila pulaṅkaḷum piṟavum potumi aṟa āṭciyiṉ uṭimi yāyiṉ, vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭattukku iṭam uṇṭākumō? uṇṭākātu. ulakil piṟanta cilarē vāḻtal vēṇṭumeṉpatā iyaṟkiyiṉ nōkkam? ellārum naṉku vāḻtal vēṇṭu meṉpatē iyaṟkiyiṉ nōkkam. ataṟkut tuṇipurital mutalāḷi āṭciyāl iyalavilliyeṉpatu veḷḷiṭi maliyeṉa viḷaṅkiviṭṭatu. attakiya āṭcimuṟi māṟutal vēṇṭumā vēṇṭāmā? māṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu colvōr mutalāḷi kūṭṭattiṉ pakivarallar; ellārum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉṉum jīvakāruṇyamuṭiyavar. avar evariṭattum pakimi koḷḷātavar. mutalāḷi āṭciyil camatarmam nilava iṭamiṉmiyāl, atu māṟuvatu nalam eṉṟu colvatu aṟamēyākum.
iyantiraṅkaḷ, uṟpattiyip perukkuvatāl mutalāḷi iṉatti ācippēy alikkap pukukiṟatu. ācippēyiṉiṉṟum viṭutaliyaṭital avviṉattāl iyalavilli. ācikkōr aḷavum uṇṭō? iyantira uṟpattikaḷ nāṭṭiṉ tēvikku mēl perukik kuvikiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl piṟanāṭṭu vāṇipa nāṭṭamum uṭaṉ perukukiṟatu. iyantira uṟpatti ōṅkiyuḷḷa ovvoru nāṭṭu mutalāḷi varkkattukkum, iyantira vācaṉi aṟiyā nāṭukaḷi - kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷi - paṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṉum āci uṇṭākiṟatu. avvāci, vāṉ pōr - nilappōr - nīrp pōrāka mūḷkiṟatu. āciyē kuṇṭu pīraṅkiyāy, naccukkāṟṟāy, piṟa kolik karuvikaḷāyp pariṇamirukkiṟatu. ennēramum caṇṭi nikaḻnta vaṇṇamirukkiṟatu. ettaṉic carva tēca caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉum, avi caṇṭiyi niṟuttum vallami peṟāmalē oḻikiṉṟaṉa. aṭippaṭi mutalāḷi varkkattiṉ āci.
kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷ iyantira mutalāḷi varkkattukku iriyāṉatum, annāṭukaḷil kālaṅ kālamāka vaḷarntuvanta kittoḻilkaḷ nācamākiṉṟaṉa. nācamākum nilimi mutalāḷi āciyāl ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. iyantiram vēṇṭāmeṉpatu eṉatu karuttaṉṟu; iyantiram potumi āṭciyilēyē makkaḷaṉi varukkum nalam purivatākumeṉpatum, cāmrājya āṭciyil iyantiram cilarkku nalaṉum palarkkuk kēṭum viḷikku meṉpatum, potumi āṭciyil iyantiram ellārkkum nalam purivatākumeṉpatum eṉatu karuttu.
cāmrājya āṭcimuṟi vaḷarntu vantāl ulakam eṉṉākum? cintikka. caṇṭi oḻital vēṇṭumā vēṇṭāmā eṉṟu āḻntu cintikka. caṇṭiyoḻiya vēṇṭumāṉāl cāmrājya āṭcimuṟi oḻital vēṇṭumaṉṟō? caṇṭi vēṇṭāmeṉṟu colvatu pāvamā?
mutalāḷi āṭciyil vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam perukuvatāl maṉpatikkaṇ oḻukkam keṭukiṟatu. vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam āṇulaki maṭṭum pīṭippatilli; peṇ ṇulakiyum pīṭikkiṟatu. ataṉāl tīyoḻukkam paravukiṟatu. iccikkāka vipacāram ceyvōr toki ciṟiyatu; mikacciṟiyatu. vaṟumiyāl vayiṟṟukkāka vipacāram ceyvōr toki periya tākiyatu; mikap periyatākiṟatu. vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam tīyoḻukkattiyum vaḷarkkiṟatu. paṇattimirāl viḷiyum tīyoḻukkaṅkaḷ kōṭi kōṭi ātaliṉ, oḻukkattiṉ poruṭṭum cāmrājya āṭci muṟi oḻital vēṇṭumeṉka.
takka maruntu
cāmrājya āṭciyi vittuk koṇṭē vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭatti oḻippateṉpatum, yuttatti niṟuttuva teṉpatum, oḻukkatti ōmpuvateṉpatum ēmāṟṟamēyākum. ēmāṟṟattukku ulakam iriyātal kūṭātu.
cāmrājya āṭci muṟiyilēyē vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭ ṭatti oḻikkavum, pōrāṭṭatti niṟuttavum, oḻukkatti ōmpavum palappala muṟikaḷ aṟiñarāl kōlappaṭṭaṉa. avi cōtikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟāl etirpārtta nalaṉ viḷiyavilli. ākavē, vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam mutaliya nōykaḷip pōkka ētēṉum takka maruntu kāṇappaṭutal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? nerukkaṭi mutira mutira vaḻituṟi viḷaṅkik koṇṭē pōkum. palappala kāṇappaṭṭu muṭivil paḻiya cama tarmamē marunteṉṟu aṟiñarāl koḷḷappaṭṭatu. paḻiya camatarmatti viññāṉat tuṇi koṇṭu putukka vēṇṭuvatu ikkāla aṟiñar kaṭami.
peyar?
āṭcimuṟip peyarip paṟṟiya kavali vēṇṭuvatilli. eppeyarāyiṉumāka; evvāṭcimuṟi āyiṉumāka atu, makkaḷ ellārum vēli ceytu piḻikkavum tīṅku viḷikkum pōrāṭṭamiṉṟi vāḻavum, oḻukkatti ōmpavum tuṇi ceyyavallatā yiruttal vēṇṭum. avvāṭcimuṟi camatarmam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ati eppeyarāl aḻittā leṉṉa? tattuvam maṭṭum piṟaḻātiruttal vēṇṭum.
camatarmam eṉpatu putiyataṉṟu. atu paṇṭik kālattil āṭciyiliruntatē. aḵtu ovvoru pōtu ovvoru vitamākak koḷḷappaṭṭu vantatu. attarmam ikkālac cāmrājya āṭcimuṟiyāl maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. ippoḻutu ulakam cāmrājyattāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
mārk
cāmrājyattāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa ulaki, viṭutali ceytu, camatarmattāl cīrtirutti amikkap piṉṉāḷil muyaṉṟavar cilar. avaruḷ ciṟantavar kāral mārk. camatarmatti viññāṉa muṟiyāl oḻuṅkupaṭutti ulakukkīnta perumi kāral mārksukku uṇṭu. mārk pōtaṉiyic cātaṉiyākka muyaṉṟavar leṉiṉ uḷḷiṭṭa cilar.
mārksiyam ulakeṅkum innāḷil ārāyappaṭukiṟatu; cintikkappaṭukiṟatu. aḵtu iṉṉum yāṇṭum muṟṟum nilavic ceykiyil nikaḻavilli. ruṣyāvil maṭṭum mārksiya viti viḻuntirukkiṟatu. aṅkē attattuvam cōtaṉiyiliruntu varukiṟatu.
mārksiyattippaṟṟi muṉṉarum eṉ karutti veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷēṉ. namatu intiya nāṭṭil mārksiyam emmuṟiyil vaḷarum eṉpatu paṟṟiyum āṅkē kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷēṉ; mēlum atupaṟṟik kūṟuvēṉ.
āti manuvum tiripum
mārksiyam tōṉṟutaṟkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē namatu nāṭṭil ātimanu (mirutimanu alla) eṉpavar tōṉṟi maṉpatikkut tarumanūl tantaṉar. attaruma nūlil piṉṉē. iṭi iṭi avvakkālattuk kēṟṟavāṟu, ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ātimanuvāl tarumattiṉ kūṟupāṭu kaḷākak kōlappeṟṟa toḻiṉ muṟikaḷ piṉṉarp piṟappu vaḻiyil nikaḻumāṟu tirikkappaṭṭaṉa. attiripuṭaṉ mēlum mēlum piṟappil uyarvu tāḻvu kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. attiripum ik kaṟpaṉiyum intiyāvi aṭimippaṭuttiṉa. ikkuṟipāṭukaḷi nīkki, ātimanuviṉ tarmatti nōkkiṉāl, atu mārksiyat tukkut tāyakamākavē viḷaṅkutal kāṇalām.
kāral mārk jermaṉiyil piṟantavar (1818 - 1883). avar pala kali payiṉṟu oru peruṅkaḻakamāyiṉar. avaratu paranta kaliñāṉam avarkkoru putumi oḷiyi vaḻaṅkiyatu. atu potumi oḷi. avvoḷiyiṉiṉṟum eḻunta eṇṇamum, pēccum, eḻuttum, muyaṟciyum akkāla jermeṉ araci naṭukkuṟac ceytaṉa. naṭukkam mārksi nāṭṭi viṭuttōṭumāṟu turattiyatu. peljiyam, pirāṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷum avarit taṅkaḷiṭam taṅkaviṭavilli. aviyum avarik kaṭintu muṭukkiṉa. iṅkilāntu mārk vatiya iṭantantatu aṅkē avar vaṟumik kaliyil mūḻkiṉar; ulaka vaṟumip piṇiyiyum, ataṟku aṭippaṭiyāyuḷḷa aṟiyāmi nōyiyum pōkka muyaṉṟaṉar; muyaṉṟu takka maruntu kaṇṭaṉar. pala maruttuvak kalikaḷ avarpāl malarntaṉa. avarāl ulakukkup potumi aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉuṭaṉ namatu nāṭṭup paṇṭi aṟamum nuḻintu oṉṟiṉāl, ulakam poruḷiṉpattuṭaṉ amiti iṉpattiyum nukarvatākum.
amiti
cāmrājyaṅkaḷukkuḷ nikaḻum pōrāṭṭam perukap peruka makkaḷukkuḷ teḷivu ēṟpaṭum. atteḷivukkut tuṇi ceyvatu mārksiyam. mārksiyattāl ellārum poruḷiṉpatti nukarum nili nēriṉum. amiti iṉpatti nukarum nili nērātu. poruḷiṉpattiṉūṭē aṟamum kalantu niṟṟal vēṇṭum. aṟaṅkalanta poruḷiṉpamē amitiyi nilipeṟutta vallatu. ataṟkuriya tuṟikaḷ intiya aṟavōr palarāl naṉku kōlappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ākavē, atu mēlum tarumatti avāviyē cīrpaṭum.
muḻu viṭutali
tarumattukku intiyā vaḻi kāṭṭutal vēṇṭum. itaṟku intiyā ippoḻutu peṟṟuḷḷa viṭutali pōtātu. atu muḻuviṭutali peṟutal vēṇṭum. muḻu viṭutalikku aṭippaṭi etu? poruḷātāra viṭutali. ituvē camatarmameṉpatu.
camatarmattukkuk kāṅkira iṇaṅkumā eṉpatu oru peruṅkēḷvi. kāṅkira ippōtu mutalāḷi vayappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu. poṟuppāṭci poṟuppāṭci eṉṟu eḻuppappeṟṟa muḻakkamellām paṇakkārarkkuc cātakamāyiṉa; ēḻi makkaṭkup pātakamāyiṉa.
cātaka pātakam eṉṉa ceykiṉṟaṉa? mutal - toḻil pōrit tuvaṅkac ceykiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉē ippōr valukkavilli. kāraṇam, anniyam āṭciyiliruntamiyē. annāḷil anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutali peṟutaṟkē kāṅkira toḻilāḷi utaviyi nāṭiyatu. toḻilāḷi kāṅkirasukkut tuṇipōṉāṉ; maṉamārat tuṇipōṉāṉ. ataṉāl anniya āṭci tolintatu. tolintatum, taṉakkuriya āṭci iṉṉum varavilliyē eṉṟu toḻilāḷi niṉikkumāṟu kāṅkira āṭci itu pōtu naṭakkiṟatu. kāṅkira cuyāṭci taṉṉik kāy vatiyum, aṭakkuvatiyum, taṉṉurimiyik kaṭiyak koṭuñ caṭṭaṅkaḷ niṟivēṟṟuvatiyum, vicāraṇi yillāmal tan talivarkaḷ ciṟikku aṉuppappaṭuvatiyum. ï‹D« ãwt‰iwí« bjhÊ yhË cz®»wh‹.: cintaṉiyil āḻkiṟāṉ. avaṉ uḷḷattil eṉṉeṉṉavō eḻukiṉṟaṉa? inniliyil kāṅkira eṉ ceytal vēṇṭum?
kāṅkira anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutali aṭintatu. iṉi atu maṟital vēṇṭum; allatu muḻu viṭutalikku muyalutal vēṇṭum. iraṇṭil oṉṟu kāṅkira cārpil nikaḻa vēṇṭumeṉṟu nāṭu etir pārkkiṟatu.
kāṅkira talivarkaḷiṟ cilar kāṅkira maṟivi virumpukiṟārilli. avar māṟutalil nāṭṭañ celuttukiṟār. māṟṟam pōṟṟaṟkuriyatē. āṉāl, māṟṟam emmuṟiyil nikaḻvatu nallatu? camatarmamuṟiyil māṟṟam nikaḻvatē nallatu. kāṅkirasi itu pōḻtu taṉ vayappaṭuttiyuḷḷa mutalāḷi immāṟṟattukku iṇaṅkuvaṉō? itu paṟṟiya karuttu vēṟṟumi kāṅkira talivarkaḷiṉ mūḷiyik kalakkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu. iyaṟki yiṉ nōkkukkum pōkkukkum araṇ ceyyum vaḻiniṉṟu kāṅkira kaṭaṉāṟṟuvatē aṟam. makātmā kānti iyaṟki vaḻiniṉṟu nalvaḻi kāṭṭuvāreṉṟu nāṭu nampukiṟatu. paṇṭita javaharlāl nēruviyum nāṭu nōkkukiṟatu.
mutal - toḻil orumippaṭak kāṅkira muyalvatu kālattukkuriya toṇṭākum. iṉi, mutal - toḻil eṉṉum īriṉam vāḻvu peṟutaṟku iṭamilli eṉṟu iyaṟki eccarikki ceyta vaṇṇamirukkiṟatu. ātaliṉ, īriṉatti ōriṉamākka muyalvatu iyaṟkiyiṉ nōkkukkum pōkkukkum tuṇi ceyva tākum.
ātināḷil mutal - toḻil eṉṟa īriṉam aṟṟa camūkamē tikaḻntatu. piṉṉē atu tirintu tirintu ippoḻutu mutal - toḻil eṉṉum īriṉamākiyatu. īriṉam paḻiyapaṭi ōriṉamāṉā laṉṟi ulakil amiti nilavātu.
vitiyiṉiṉṟum muḷi eḻukiṟatu. muḷi ceṭiyākiṟatu. ceṭi maramākiṟatu. maram ñāyiṟākiṟatō? tiṅkaḷākiṟatō? illi. piṉṉi eṉṉa ākiṟatu? maram kaṉi viṭukiṟatu. kaṉiyi ṉiṉṟum paḻiya vitiyē tōṉṟukiṟatu. atē pōlac camūkam īriṉam aṟṟa oṉṟiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyatu. atu mīṇṭum paḻiya niliyiyē eytum; eytiyē tīrum, iḵtu iyaṟki. iyaṟkikkut tuṇipōvatē aṟam - ahimsi.
īriṉam eḷitil ōriṉamākumā? āyiṉāl nallatē. illiyēl puraṭci eḻum puraṭci aṟavaḻi nikaḻtal vēṇṭum. ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum puraṭci matavaḻi nikaḻtalākātu. kāntiyam namakku nalvaḻi kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. aḵtu anniya āṭciyi eppaṭit tolittatu? ahimsiyālaṉṟō tolittatu? anta ahimsiyiyē īriṉatti ōriṉamākkuvataṟkuṅ kōṭal poruttam. ahimsiyē intiyat tarumam. ataṟkuk kēṭu cūḻalākātu.
īriṉam oḻinta camatarumatti innāḷil viññāṉa muṟiyil aṟivuṟuttiyavar kāral mārk. ammārksiyamum nam kāntiyamum oṉṟik kaṭaṉāṟṟiṉāl intiyat tarumattik kāttal kūṭum. iraṇṭuṅkalanta oṉṟē namatu nāṭṭukkut tēvi eṉṟu pala āṇṭāka yāṉ valiyuṟutti varuvatu nāṭṭavarkkut teriyum.
mārksiyamum kāntiyamum cērnta oṉṟiyē yāṉ caṉmārkkam eṉṟu pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṟēṉ. catteṉuñ cemporuḷi uṇarttuvatu kāntiyameṉṟum, ati aṭiyum mārkkattik kāṭṭuvatu mārksiyameṉṟuṅ koḷka. muḻu viṭutalikku - poruḷātāra viṭutalikku - camatarmattukkuc ceyyappaṭum muyaṟciyiṉaṭiyil ahimsi vērkoṇṭu niṟṟal avaciyam eṉṟu aṟikūvukiṟēṉ.
tiṭṭam
camatarmattik kuṟikkōḷāk koṇṭu kāṅkira cila tiṭṭaṅkaḷik kōlum muyaṟciyil talippaṭuvatu ciṟappu. namatu nāṭu nīṇṭakālamāka anniya āṭcikku iriyāṉamiyāl, atu nāṭṭukkuriya kalikaḷuṭaṉ peritun toṭarpu koḷḷā toḻintatu. nāṭṭuk kalikaḷ nāṭṭil mīṇṭum uyirt teḻuntāl camatarmam makkaṭkup putumiyākat tōṉṟātu; atu paḻamiyākavē tōṉṟum. intiyak kaliñāṉam iṭiyil piṟaḻntupaṭṭamiyāl nāṭṭil cātimatap piṇakkukaḷ nāṭṭip palavaḻiyilum alittaṉa; arittaṉa; erittaṉa. av valivu, arippu, erivu māyntu pōka uḻittal vēṇṭum. cātimatak kuṟumpukaḷit tolippataṟkup pala vaḻi kūṟalām. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟic ciṟappāka muṉṉaruṅ kūṟiṉēṉ. īṇṭuṅ kuṟikkiṟēṉ. atu kalappumaṇam. kalappu maṇattāl cātimatap puṉmi poṉṟuvatu tiṇṇam. 
nāṭṭu vaḷarccikkuk karuttu vēṟṟumi iṉṟiyamiyātatu. karuttu vēṟṟumikku iṭantārāta nāṭu vaḷarcciyuṟātu ciṟippaṭṭuc cāmpum. karuttu vēṟṟumiyik kāyātu kaṭiyātu ataṟku iṭantaruvatu vaḷarcciyi nāṭuvatākum. namatu nāṭṭil muṉṉāḷil naṉṉeṟikaḷ pala vaḷarntē vantaṉa. vaḷarccikkuk kāraṇam annāḷil karuttu vēṟṟumikku iṭantantamiyē yākum.  ivvaṟa voḻukkam iṭikkālattil vīḻntatu. ataṉāl nāṭu kālattukkēṟṟa vaḷarcci peṟāmal tēṅkiyatu. iṭik kālattil iṟaṅkiya nañcu - iṉṉum ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭi lum - pala nāṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavu peṟṟa innāḷilum - anniya āṭciyāl kaṭṭuṇṭu oruvita viṭutalipeṟṟa ivvēḷiyilum - oḻiyavilli. karuttu vēṟṟumi kāraṇamāka oruvari oruvar pakittal aṟiyāmi; iḻivu; nīcam; caṇṭāḷam. karuttu vēṟṟumikku iṭantaruvatu camatarma vaḷarccikku iṭantaruvatākum. karuttu vēṟṟumikku iṭantarum aṟivukkali vaḷarvataṟkut talivarkaḷ naṭantu nalvaḻi kāṭṭi uḻikka muṟpaṭuvārkaḷāka.
jaṉanāyaka āṭci makkaḷiṉ vākkiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu amivatu āṭcikku mūlamāyiruppatu vākku, attakiya vākku ettakiya poṟuppuṭiyatā yiruttal vēṇṭum? iti maṉpati uṇarntu naṭakkuṅ kālamē aṟavaḷarccik kuriyatākum.
vākku, tūya urimiyiṉiṉṟum eḻum teyvīkamuṭiyatu. atit teyvakkoṭi eṉṟuṅ koḷḷalām. av vākki nalvaḻiyil celutta vēṇṭuvatu makkaḷ kaṭami. atu vēṟu vaḻiyil celuttappaṭiṉ, jaṉanāyakam pōliyāy, nañcāy nāṭṭi erippatākum.
namatu intiya nāṭu toṉmi vāyntatu; carittira kālattukku muṉṉarē nākarikattiṟ paṇpaṭṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. maliyiṉ māṇpiyum kaṭaliṉ ciṟappiyum uṇarntatu; kāviya ōviyaṅ kaḷik kaṇṭatu; vāḻkkit tattuvattil teḷivu peṟṟatu. ippeṟṟi vāynta nāṭu iṭikkālattil cātimata vēṟṟumi kaḷāl pala tiṟak kuḻappaṅkaṭku iriyākiyatu. ataṉāl atu nāḷukkunāḷ aṭimiyākiyākit taṉatu nalliyalpukaḷi yellām iḻantatu.
ēṟakkuṟiya irunūṟāṇṭu namatu intiya nāṭu piriṭṭiṣā rāl āḷappaṭṭatu; avartam nākarika vaḻiyē araciyal payiṉṟatu; jaṉanāyakatti vēṭṭatu. itu pōḻtu jaṉanāyakattukkuriya oruvita viṭutaliyiyum peṟṟatu. ivviṭutaliyi muḻumiya tākkum poṟuppu nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉē niṟkiṟatu.
muḻu viṭutalikkup palappala iyalpukaḷ tēvi. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu vākkurimiyi nalvaḻiyiṟ celuttuvatu. ivviyalpu namatu nāṭṭil enniliyil uḷḷatu? itaṟku viṭi iṟukkavallatu anupavam, anupavam iṉṉateṉpatic caṭṭa capikaḷ aṟivuṟuttā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
namatu nāṭṭil peritum cātimata veṟikaḷum, paṇam, tayi tāṭcaṇyam mutaliya māyikaḷum vākkāyp pariṇamittuc caṭṭacapikaḷātal veḷḷiṭimali, pōtiya kalviyaṟivu, araciyal ñāṉam mutaliyaṉa vāykkap peṟāta makkaḷ vākku eṅṅaṉam urimiyuṇarviṉiṉṟum eḻum?
innāḷil vayatuṟṟavarellārukkum vākkurimi kiṭit tuḷḷatu. itu pōṟṟaṟkuriyatē. āṉāl vākkurimi nāṭṭil cenneṟiyil celuttappaṭumā eṉṟu aṟiñar ulakam aiyuṟukiṟatu. curuṅkiya muṟiyil muṉṉē kiṭitta vākkurimi cenneṟiyil celuttappaṭāmiyē aiyattukkuk kāraṇamāy niṟkiṟatu pōlum!
vākkāḷar cinti araciyaṟ potumiyiṟ cellumāṟum, cāti matam paṇam tayi tāṭcaṇyam mutaliya māyikaḷil cellātavāṟum kākka valla kalviyum araciyal ñāṉamum avarkkut tēvi. ittēviyi niṟivu ceyyak kāṅkira muyalvatu nallatu. vākkāḷar maṉōnili cīrpaṭak kāṅkira piracārañ ceyvatāka; āṅkāṅkē araciyaṟpaḷḷi amittu makkaḷukkup pōtaṉi ceyvatāka. kāṅkira taṉ kaṭaṉi ittuṟiyil āṟṟātoḻiyiṉ, caṭṭacapikaḷil tēḷ pāmpu karaṭi, puli mutaliyaṉa ulavutal nērum. ākavē, kāṅkira taṉ poṟuppi uṇarntu naṭappatāka.
makkaḷāṭci vaḷartaṟku orēvita araciyal kaṭci nilavutal kūṭātu. atu pāsisamāy nāṭṭi iṭarppaṭuttum; jaṉanāya kattik kollum. ākavē, palatiṟak karuttuṭiya araciyal kaṭcikaḷ amivatē ciṟappu. cāti mataṅkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa kaṭcikaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, cātimataṅkaṭkeṉṟu pīṭaṅkaḷi otukkal mutaliyaṉa nāṭṭic ciṟumip paṭuttum: ciṟippaṭuttum. cātimatam mutaliya ciṟumikaḷaṟṟa kaṭcikaḷē nāṭṭi vaḷarkkum. nāṭṭiṉ vaḷarccikku itukāṟum pāṭupaṭṭu oruvita viṭutaliyi nāṭṭukku nalkiya kāṅkira, iṉi nalla araciyal kaṭcikaḷ amiyavum tuṇi ceyvatāka; kāṅkira oru kaṭciyāka niṉṟu kaṭaṉāṟṟātiruppatāka.
ikkāla nākarikak kuḻavikaḷ cila. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu pattiriki! intiyap pattirikiyulakam iṉṉum naṉmuṟiyil paṇpaṭavilli. intiyap pattiriki ulakam peritum vakuppu vātattukku ūṟṟāka niṟṟal veḷḷiṭimali nāṭṭil viḷintuvarum vakuppuppūcal - piṇakku - pōr - koli - koḷḷi mutaliya koṭumikaṭkellām mūla kāraṇaṅkaḷāka niṟpaṉavaṟṟuḷ pattiriki ulakamē ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. anniya āṭcivilakiya nāḷtoṭṭu, nāṭṭup pattirikikaḷiṟ perum pāṉmiyaṉa, mutalāḷiyip pōṟṟiyum toḻilāḷiyit tūṟṟiyum eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉa; kāṅkira āṭci cārpil nikaḻun tavaṟutalkaḷik kaṭṭuppāṭāka maṟittu varukiṉṟaṉa. taṉittaṉi maṉitarit tākkutal, poymikaḷip parapputal, vacimoḻi kaḷic corital, vakuppup pōrkaḷit tūṇṭal mutaliya ciṟumikaḷ malintu miṭiyum pattirikip perukkaliṉāl nāṭu naṉṉili eytumā? intiyap pattirikiyulakam camatarmatti ātarikka uṟutikoḷḷutal vēṇṭum; koṇṭāl pala tuṟikaḷ cīrpaṭum. pattirikic cīrtiruttattukkeṉṟu oru taṉi iyakkam tōṉṟiṉum ati nāṭu varavēṟkum.
kāṅkira āṭci aṭakkumuṟic caṭṭaṅkaḷi oḻikka iyalātu, putupputu aṭakkumuṟic caṭṭaṅkaḷi niṟuva muyalvatu, muḻu viṭutaliyik kuṟikkoṇṭu uḻippatākātu. aṭakku muṟic caṭṭaṅkaḷ koli kaḷavu poy mutaliyavaṟṟi vaḷarkkum maṟakkaruvikaḷ, koṭuñ caṭṭaṅkaḷāl nāṭṭi arippatu nākarikamākātu. accaṭṭaṅkaḷ nākarika makkaḷiyum anākarika mākkaḷākkum. kāṅkira āṭci caṭṭaṅkaḷic curukkic curukki aṟattip perukka muṉivatu camatarmattukkuk kāl koḷvatākum.
reyil, ṭirām, pa, ciṉimā, pāṅk, toḻiṟ cālikaḷ mutaliyaṉa taṉi maṉitar vayappaṭāmal, potumi araciṉ vayappaṭumāṟum, avvāṟē nilapulaṅkaḷum, piṟavum potumiyākumāṟum kāṅkira tiṭṭaṅ kōlic camatarmatti vaḷarkka eḻuvatāka. ellām camatarma mayamāṉāl pōlī, paṭṭāḷam, kolik karuvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa eṟṟukku? mutalil kāṅkira camatarma vaḻiyil māṟṟam peṟat talivarkaḷ toṇṭāṟṟuvārkaḷāka. intiyāvil camatarmam, mārksiya - kāntiya vaḻiyē eḻuvatāka, etaṟkum puraṭci tēvi. puraṭci aṟavaḻi nikaḻvatāka. maṟappuraṭci vēṇṭā; vēṇṭā; ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum vēṇṭā. kāṅkira camatarmattik kaṭippiṭittoḻuka āṇṭavaṉ aruḷ ceyvāṉāka. ellārum iṉpuṟ ṟirukka niṉippatuvē - allāmal vēṟoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parāparamē eṉpatu tāyumāṉār tiruvākku.

muṭivuri
innūlil mūvaki intiyāvum mūvaki rājyamum pēcappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa; muṟkāla intiyāvum iṭikkāla intiyāvum, hintu rājyamum mulim rājyamum eppaṭi vaḷarntu tēyntaṉa eṉpatum, ikkāla intiyā ekkāṭci vaḻaṅkukiṟatu eṉpatum, intiyāvil piriṭṭil āṭci evvāṟu nuḻintatu eṉpatum viritturikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṭṭi aṭimippaṭutti yavi iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭa taṉi vāḻkki, piṟappil uyarvu tāḻvu karutal, mataveṟi, peṇṇaṭimi, tīṇṭāmi, kaṇmūṭi vaḻakka oḻukkac campiratāyaṅkaḷ, cakōtara nēyamiṉmi, kāṭṭik koṭuttal, hintu - mulim vēṟṟumi mutaliyaṉa eṉṟum, ikkuṟipāṭukaḷ anniya āṭciyik kūvi aḻittaṉa eṉṟum, ippoḻutu kiṭittuḷḷa viṭutali muḻumiyatākātu eṉṟum, iṉip poruḷātāra viṭutali muyaṟci virinteḻal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, ammuyaṟciyilēyē peruṅkavali celuttal vēṇṭum  eṉṟum, mutalil kāṅkira camatarma mayamātal vēṇṭum eṉṟum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
muḻuviṭutali muyaṟci eṉṟatum pirañcup puraṭci ruṣyap puraṭci mutaliya maṟappuraṭcikaḷ mītu cilar eṇṇañ cellum. maṟappuraṭci pōri vaḷarppatu; tēyppataṉṟu. veṭṭukiṟavaṉ veṭṭappaṭuvāṉ eṉpatu kiṟitu perumāṉ perumoḻi, piṉṉi etu nallatu? aṟappuraṭciyē nallatu. intiya muṉivarāl aṟivuṟuttappeṟṟatu aṟappuraṭciyē.
eṇṇam
aṟappuraṭcikkuk kolik karuvikaḷ tēvi illi. ataṟku nalliyalpukaḷ tēvi. nalliyalpukaḷ amitaṟkup palappala muṟikaḷ aṟavōrāl kōlappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟi aṟanūlkaḷiṟ pārkka. īṇṭu oṉṟic ciṟappāka valiyuṟutta virumpukiṟēṉ.
emmuyaṟcikkum aṭippaṭi eṇṇam. eṇṇam pēccā kiṟatu; ceyalākiṟatu. eṇṇam iruvakiyatu. oruvaki, puṟamaṉatti ṉiṉṟum piṟappatu; maṟṟoru vaki, uḷmaṉattiṉiṉṟum piṟappatu. iraṇṭuḷ piṉṉiyatē ciṟantatu.
puṟa (maṉa) eṇṇamum, ataṉiṉṟeḻum pēccum ceyalum taṉṉalatti vaḷarppaṉavāy, mūrkka caktiyi eḻuppuvaṉavāyp pōrāṭṭattip perukkuvaṉavām. uḷ (maṉa) eṇṇam pēccākavum ceyalākavuṅkūṭa eḻa vēṇṭuvatilli. av veṇṇamē āṟṟal vāyntatu; avvāṟṟal aḷapparitu. atu taṉṉalaṅ kaṭantatu; āṉmacakti vāyntatu. pīraṅki kuṇṭuka ḷālum accaktiyi aḻittal iyalātu.
tavam
uḷ uṇarvāl oṉṟi eṇṇi uṉṉi muṉṉuvatu āḻnta cintaṉi allatu tavam eṉṉappaṭum. maṉivi makkaḷi valintu nīttuk kāṭṭukkōṭi mūkkip piṭittu mūcci aṭakkuvatu tavamākātu. tavamāvatu, akavuṇarvip paṇpaṭutti, atil oṉṟi āḻnta cintaṉiyil amarvatu. itu cintaṉākāram. ippayiṟciyil nammuṉṉōr mikattērcci peṟṟavarāyiruntaṉar. ataṟkeṉṟē avartaṅ kalvi, celvam, toḻil, uṇavu, uṭi, vāḻkki, aracu mutaliyaṉa payaṉpaṭṭu vantaṉa. innāḷil akavuṇarvu nuṭpam mēlnāṭṭil akattiṇiyar (Psychologists) cilariṭam viḷaṅki varukiṟatu. atu nāḷaṭivil aṅkē ākkam peṟalām.
āḻnta cintaṉikkup puṟa maṉa oṭukkam vēṇṭum. ataṟkup pulaṉaṭakkam iṉṟiyamiyātatu. pulaṉaṭakkattukku nalloḻukkam tēvi. nalloḻukkam eḷiya aṟa vāḻkkiyil arumpum.
1pulaṉaṭakkam piṉṉāḷil pulaṉaḻivākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. itu tiripu. iti, peṇṇiṉ perumi, murukaṉ allatu aḻaku mutaliya nūlkaḷil viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. 
eḷiya aṟa vāḻkki viḻumiya nōkkatti vaḷarkka vallatu, eḷiya vāḻkkiyum viḻumiya nōkkamum namatu nāṭṭuc celvam, accelvam eṅkē? pērācip pēy vāḻkkiyum taṉṉala nōkkamumallavō innāḷil nāṭṭi arikkiṉṟaṉa?
eḷiya vāḻkkiyāl pala nalaṉkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantaṉa cutēciya ākkamum ahimsi nōkkamumākum. iraṇṭum meymmi viṭutalikku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. avaṟṟik koṇṭu, makātmā kānti periya cāmrājyatti etirttu, aṟappōr purintu, oruvita viṭutali peṟṟatu ulakaṟintatoṉṟu. ahimsi aṟṟa iṭattil maṟappōr - maṟappuraṭci - nikaḻntu koṇṭē pōkum.
intiya muḻu viṭutalikku - camatarma āṭcikku - aṟap puraṭciyē cālacciṟantatu. appuraṭciyāl muṟkāla intiyā taṟkālap pōrviyuṭaṉ uyirtteḻum. avvintiyā, himsi mayamāka uḷḷa ikkāla ulaka viṭutalikkum vaḻi kāṭṭuvatākum. ātalāl, mutalil vēṇṭaṟpālatu intiya muḻu viṭutali atāvatu camatarmam. avviṭutali vēṭkiyi eḻuppuvatu innūl.
tiru. vi. kaliyāṇacuntaraṉ

1.	ātimanu jinar eṉṟu jina nūlkaḷ colkiṉṟaṉa; tirāviṭareṉṟa campiratāyamum uṇṭu.
2.	jinam taṉ koḷkiyit tarmam eṉṟē kūṟukiṟatu.

1.	‘Rivers, water - courses, running through the village, ponds large and small, burning - places, were free to all. Every village had a comparatively large space of land round it for pasture. This, as well as lands which were entirely barren and uncultivable, were also common property On lands which were not cultivated any villager had the right to graze his cattle. On lands which were cultivated after the harvest. Before the next cultivating season, the same right of grazing existed. Everybody had an equal right to cut jungle fire 	- wood for fuel, and take jungle produce for manure, and to take timber for building his house. This right was only limited and generally regulated by the village assembly. The rights in cultivable lands varied with the type of village. The village, or the rights in the village, were vested in the settlers as a collective body.*** By this means they effectully excluded the right of any proprietor to any particular spot and established the clim of the general body to the Whole village in common’	- T.Rangachari

1.  toḻilāḷar caṅkaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅkē nāṭōṟun tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi vaḻi tuṟiyillāmal vaḷarttu varuvatāl, pala iṭarkaḷ iṭiyil nērntuviṭum. ātalāl, ellāt toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaṭkum naṭu nāyakamāka oru mattiyac caṅkamiruttal vēṇṭum. accaṅkattil ellāt toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷuñ cērtal vēṇṭum. mattiyac caṅkam oṉṟiruppatu toḻilāḷar iyakkattukku ākkantēṭuvatākum. oru mattiyac caṅkam amikka srīmati mirunāḷiṉi caṭōpāttiyāyā ceytuvarum muyaṟcikku immakānāṭu tuṇi ceyyumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
1. 	To
	KALYANASUNDARA MUDALIYAR, ESQ. Madras.
My dear friend,
 	This is your book as much as mine. If the speeches delivered here achieved any good among our friends at Perambur it is due to your excellent
	translations of them. What could I have done in the Labour work in this city without you? You translated my speeches - not my words merely but their very spirit. We have rejoiced and suffered together with thousands. Your unobtrusive manner, your loyal support. Your sagacious advice have been priceless. Let me request you to accept this small gift of dedication for your all too splendid services. 			Ever yours, 				B.P. WADIA.,
(‘Labour in Madras’ by B.P.Wadia with a foreword by Col. Josiah C. Wedg-wood. M.P., (1921). S’ Ganesan Co., Publishers, Triplicane, Madras, S.E.)
	“.......All these parties were present on the 10th August except the Madras Labour Union President who addressed the Committee the following letter:-
	Sir,
	I have the honour to acknowledge the receipt of your letter dated the 6th inst. relating to the sittings of the committee of inquiry into the Madras Disturbances of June and July 1921 and to thank you for inviting me to be present at the sid inquiry.
	In reply I am desired by the Madras Labour Union to inform you that the Madras Labour Union as such does not propose to participate in the inquiry which as the Government order points out, will not extend to the merits and to any disputes between the managements of the Buckingham and Carnatic Mills and their employees. The inquiry into the disturbances which took place in the Perambur division on and after the 29th June, is a matter of concern to the general public and not to the Members of the Union as such, and it affects the peace and order of northern parts of the city. The Labour Union repudiates any manner of connection with such disturbances as have taken place. They have since been in part at least a matter of adjudication before the Magisterial Courts in Madras and evidence has been adduced before these courts at the instance of the police as well as the parties aggrieved and judgements have been pronounced by the Chief Presidency Magistrate of the city in two cases which show clearly the origin of the disturbance and the persons involved in provoking them as well as the conduct of the police in dealing with them. There are still a number of police proceedings pending before stipendiary and honorary magistrates in respect of this matter, in which Members of the Union are being needlessly harassed and put to great hardship. In the circumstances,
	the Union is unable to furnish the Committee with a list of persons who might be examined as wittnesses by your committee and I regret very much to find myself unable to supply you now or later with a list of persons whom I could in the existing state of things suggest for examination as witnesses.
		Yours truly,
	T.V.KALYANASUNDARAM
	President, Madras Labour Union.
	   In consequence of this refusal on the part of the Labour Union to participate in the inquiry which was much regretted by the committee, the committee announced at the first meeting that they desired it to be known that any accredited representatives of the strikers would be affored the same facilities in regard to this inquiry as would have been given to the Madras Labour Union. The representatives of the Press were asked to give every possible publicity to this announcement. But unfortunately no representative of the strikers or any section of them appeared during the inquiry......”			- “Hindu” (14-10-1921)
(G.O.No.671, 7th October 1921, Madras Disturbances july - 1921)
